2}=\tfrac{1}{2}a_{\pm 2}(c_{m}^{\pm}c_{m}^{\pm}-\tfrac{1}{r}c_{m}^{\pm})\mathchoice{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}=\tfrac{1}{2}a_{\pm 2}c_{m\pm 1}^{\pm}c_{m}^{\pm}\mathchoice{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\,.

(5.29)

5.1
The Radial Equations

Using separation of variables, the wave equations just derived are reduced to radial equations.
We give explicit solutions Bessel equations arising this way.

Central Mode

Using the decomposition (
5.24
), the inhomogeneous wave equation (
5.22
) yields

\displaystyle\mathrm{B}^{\pm}_{0}\mathchoice{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}{\pm}}}}{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}{\pm}}}}{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}{\pm}}}}{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}{\pm}}}}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{4}a_{0}(\zeta/\rho^{2}-2\beta^{2})f_{0}(\alpha,r)\,,

(5.30)

where

\displaystyle\mathrm{B}^{\pm}_{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial r^{2}}+\frac{1}{r}\frac{\partial}{\partial r}+\frac{\zeta_{\pm}}{\rho^{2}}-\frac{m^{2}}{r^{2}}\,,

(5.31)

\displaystyle\zeta_{\pm}

\displaystyle=\rho^{2}(n^{2}\omega_{\pm}^{2}-\beta_{\pm}^{2})\,,

(5.32)

\displaystyle\omega_{\pm}

\displaystyle=\omega\pm\omega_{g}\,,

(5.33)

\displaystyle\beta_{\pm}

\displaystyle=\beta\pm\mathchoice{{\hat{\kappa}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\parallel}}}}{{\hat{\kappa}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\parallel}}}}{{\hat{\kappa}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\parallel}}}}{{\hat{\kappa}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\parallel}}}}\omega_{g}\,.

(5.34)

The homogeneous solutions to this equation which are regular at the origin and vanish at infinity are evidently

f_{0}^{\pm}(\sigma,r)=\begin{cases}\sigma_{1}J_{m}(U_{\pm}r/\rho)\,,&r<\rho\,,\\
\sigma_{2}K_{m}(W_{\pm}r/\rho)\,,&r>\rho\,,\end{cases}

(5.35)

where
\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}
are constants (continuity conditions relating them are discussed in a later section), and where
U_{\pm},W_{\pm}
are defined in analogy to
U,W
in (
4.13
):

U_{\pm}=\sqrt{\rho^{2}(n_{1}^{2}\omega_{\pm}^{2}-\beta_{\pm}^{2})}\,,\qquad\text{and}\qquad W_{\pm}=\sqrt{\rho^{2}(\beta_{\pm}^{2}-n_{2}^{2}\omega_{\pm}^{2})}\,.

(5.36)

To find particular solutions, we consider the more general problem

\rho^{2}\mathrm{B}^{\pm}_{\nu}p_{\nu}^{\pm}=\begin{cases}\alpha_{1}J_{m+\nu}(Ur/\rho)\,,&r<\rho\,,\\
\alpha_{2}K_{m+\nu}(Wr/\rho)\,,&r>\rho\,,\end{cases}

(5.37)

where
\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}
are arbitrary constants and
\mathrm{B}^{\pm}_{\nu}
is a Bessel operator of arbitrary order
\nu
:

\mathrm{B}^{\pm}_{\nu}=\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial r^{2}}+\frac{1}{r}\frac{\partial}{\partial r}+\frac{\zeta_{\pm}}{\rho^{2}}-\frac{(m+\nu)^{2}}{r^{2}}\,.

(5.38)

Particular solutions to this equation are given by

\displaystyle p_{\nu}^{\pm}(\alpha,r)

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\alpha_{1}[Y_{m+\nu}(U_{\pm}r/\rho)\Gamma^{\pm}_{m+\nu}(r/\rho)+J_{m+\nu}(U_{\pm}r/\rho)\tilde{\Gamma}^{\pm}_{m+\nu}(r/\rho)]\,,&r<\rho\,,\\
\alpha_{2}[I_{m+\nu}(W_{\pm}r/\rho)\Delta^{\pm}_{m+\nu}(r/\rho)+K_{m+\nu}(W_{\pm}r/\rho)\tilde{\Delta}^{\pm}_{m+\nu}(r/\rho)]\,,&r>\rho\,,\end{cases}

(5.39)

where

\displaystyle\Gamma_{\nu}^{\pm}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi}{2}\int_{0}^{z}J_{\nu}(U_{\pm}z^{\prime})J_{\nu}(Uz^{\prime})z^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}\,,

(5.40)

\displaystyle\tilde{\Gamma}_{\nu}^{\pm}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi}{2}\int_{z}^{1}Y_{\nu}(U_{\pm}z^{\prime})J_{\nu}(Uz^{\prime})z^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}\,,

(5.41)

\displaystyle\Delta_{\nu}^{\pm}(z)

\displaystyle=-\int_{z}^{\infty}K_{\nu}(W_{\pm}z^{\prime})K_{\nu}(Wz^{\prime})z^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}\,,

(5.42)

\displaystyle\tilde{\Delta}_{\nu}^{\pm}(z)

\displaystyle=-\int_{1}^{z}I_{\nu}(W_{\pm}z^{\prime})K_{\nu}(Wz^{\prime})z^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}\,.

(5.43)

Equation (
5.37
) is readily verified using the Wrońskians

\begin{vmatrix}J_{m}(r)&J_{m}^{\prime}(r)\\
Y_{m}(r)&Y_{m}^{\prime}(r)\end{vmatrix}=\frac{2}{\pi}\frac{1}{r}\,,\qquad\text{and}\qquad\begin{vmatrix}K_{m}(r)&K_{m}^{\prime}(r)\\
I_{m}(r)&I_{m}^{\prime}(r)\end{vmatrix}=\frac{1}{r}\,,

(5.44)

cf. e.g. Ref.
[
36
, eqns. 9.1.15 on p. 360 and 9.6.15 on p. 375]
.
The integral ranges were chosen as follows.
The function
\Gamma_{\nu}^{\pm}
multiplies
Y_{\nu}
which diverges at the origin, so we chose the region of integration such that
\Gamma_{\nu}^{\pm}
vanishes there.
The region of integration for
\Delta_{\nu}^{\pm}
was chosen for a similar reason, as
I_{\nu}
diverges at large distances.
Finally, the ranges for
\tilde{\Gamma}_{\nu}^{\pm}
and
\tilde{\Delta}_{\nu}^{\pm}
were chosen such that these functions vanish at the core-cladding interface
r=\rho
, where certain components of the electromagnetic field are required to be continuous.

Using the function
p_{0}
, one obtain the general solution to (
5.30
) which is regular at the origin and vanishes at infinity:

\mathchoice{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}{\pm}}}}{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}{\pm}}}}{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{0}{\pm}}}}{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.98613pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][ (5.45)

where
a_{0}
was factored out for convenience.

(5.48)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[12.05713pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.05713pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.26111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.63333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.16666pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\pm \displaystyle=a_{\pm 1}\left(\tfrac{i}{\sqrt{2}}\rho\beta\,p^{-}_{\pm 1}(\mp U\alpha_{1},-W\alpha_{2},r)+f^{-}_{\pm 1}(\sigma_{\pm 1,-},r)\right)\,,

(5.49)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[12.05713pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.05713pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.26111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\pm 2}{+}}}}

\displaystyle=a_{\pm 2}\left(\tfrac{1}{4}p^{+}_{\pm 2}(U^{2}\alpha_{1},W^{2}\alpha_{2},r)+f^{+}_{\pm 2}(\sigma_{\pm 2,+},r)\right)\,,

(5.50)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[12.05713pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.05713pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.26111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.63333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.16666pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\pm 2}{-}}}}

\displaystyle=a_{\pm 2}\left(\tfrac{1}{4}p^{-}_{\pm 2}(U^{2}\alpha_{1},W^{2}\alpha_{2},r)+f^{-}_{\pm 2}(\sigma_{\pm 2,-},r)\right)\,,

(5.51)

where the various
\sigma
’s are parameters to be determined from continuity conditions, and the functions
f^{\pm}_{k}
are derived from
f^{\pm}_{0}
, defined in (
5.35
), by successive application of the ladder operators
c_{m}^{\pm}
defined in (
4.11
):

\displaystyle f^{+}_{\pm 1}

\displaystyle=\rho c_{m}^{\pm}f^{+}_{0}\,,

\displaystyle f^{+}_{\pm 2}

\displaystyle=\rho^{2}c_{m\pm 1}^{\pm}c_{m}^{\pm}f^{+}_{0}\,,

(5.52)

\displaystyle f^{-}_{\pm 1}

\displaystyle=\rho c_{m}^{\pm}f^{-}_{0}\,,

\displaystyle f^{-}_{\pm 2}

\displaystyle=\rho^{2}c_{m\pm 1}^{\pm}c_{m}^{\pm}f^{-}_{0}\,.

(5.53)

Having found the longitudinal components of the electromagnetic field, one can now compute the remaining (transverse) components.

5.2
The Transverse Components

To obtain the transverse components of
Z
, consider the
\pm
components of (
3.9
).
Expanding
\mathrm{g}=\mathrm{g}^{(0)}+\varepsilon\mathrm{g}^{(1)}
,
Z=Z^{(0)}+\varepsilon Z^{(1)}
, and using the unperturbed equation, one obtains at first order

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{2}}a_{+1}c_{m+1}^{+}\mathchoice{Z^{{{{(0)}}{+}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{+}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{+}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{+}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\,,

\displaystyle\chi^{-}_{-1}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{2}}a_{-1}c_{m-1}^{-}\mathchoice{Z^{{{{(0)}}{-}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.63333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.16666pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{-}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.63333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.16666pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{-}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.63333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.16666pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{-}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.63333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.16666pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\,,

(5.60)

\displaystyle\chi^{+}_{+2}

\displaystyle=0\,,

\displaystyle\chi^{-}_{-2}

\displaystyle=0\,.

(5.61)

To arrive at the stated formula for
\chi^{\pm}_{0}
, we have used
\mathfrak{e}_{\mp}(Z^{{(0)}\parallel})=-i(\beta\mp ijn\omega)Z^{{(0)}\pm}
which follows from (
4.9
), and the trace condition (
5.12
).
For
\chi^{+}_{-1}
and
\chi^{-}_{+1}
we have used

\rho^{2}c_{m+1}^{-}c_{m}^{+}f_{0}=\rho^{2}c_{m-1}^{+}c_{m}^{-}f_{0}=-\zeta f_{0}\,,

(5.62)

which can be shown either using the recursion relations (
4.15
) or by noting that
c_{m+1}^{-}c_{m}^{+}=c_{m-1}^{+}c_{m}^{-}=\mathrm{B}_{0}-\zeta/\rho^{2}
and using the Bessel equation
\mathrm{B}_{0}f_{0}=0
.
These formulae have the general structure that the radial dependence of
\chi^{a}_{b}
is proportional to
a_{b}f_{a+b}
.

Using the notation (
5.24
), as well as the abbreviations
\omega_{\pm}
and
\beta_{\pm}
as defined in (
5.33
) and (
5.34
), equation (
5.55
) reduces to

ijn\omega_{-}](c_{m+k}^{\pm}\mathchoice{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.63333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.16666pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{-}}}}{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.63333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.16666pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{-}}}}{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.63333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.16666pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{-}}}}{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.69446 (5.66)

which is structurally similar to the unperturbed equation (
4.9
).

Now that the field equations are solved in the core and cladding separately, we consider how these solutions match at the core-cladding interface of the waveguide.

5.3
Continuity Conditions

From (
3.3
), one finds that the field components
D^{r},E_{\theta},E_{z},B^{r},H_{\theta},H_{z}
must be continuous at the core-cladding interface
r=\rho
.
As is well-known, these six continuity conditions are linearly dependent, and it suffices to impose continuity of the four components

\displaystyle D^{r}

\displaystyle=\Re Z^{r}\,,

\displaystyle B^{r}

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\Im Z^{r}\,,

\displaystyle H_{\parallel}

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\mathchoice{\mathrm{g}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\parallel}{i}}}}{\mathrm{g}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\parallel}{i}}}}{\mathrm{g}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\parallel}{i}}}}{\mathrm{g}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\parallel}{i}}}}\Im Z^{i}\,,

\displaystyle E_{\parallel}

\displaystyle=n^{-2}\mathchoice{\mathrm{g}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\parallel}{i}}}}{\mathrm{g}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\parallel}{i}}}}{\mathrm{g}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\parallel}{i}}}}{\mathrm{g}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\parallel}{i}}}}\Re Z^{i}\,,

(5.67)

where
\Re
and
\Im
refer to the
j
-real and
j
-imaginary parts, respectively.
To assess the jumps of the fields, we introduce the notation

\left\llbracket{f}\right\rrbracket:=\lim_{r\nearrow\rho}f(r)-\lim_{r\searrow\rho}f(r)\,,

(5.68)

and write symbolically

\left\llbracket{Z}\right\rrbracket=\begin{pmatrix}\left\llbracket{D^{r}}\right\rrbracket&\left\llbracket{E_{\parallel}}\right\rrbracket&\left\llbracket{B^{r}}\right\rrbracket&\left\llbracket{H_{\parallel}}\right\rrbracket\end{pmatrix}^{\mathsf{T}}\,,

(5.69)

which conflates the jumps of all relevant fields in one
column
vector.

Since the jumps arising in (
5.67
) are determined by the jumps of the two components

\displaystyle\mathchoice{Z^{{{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{2}}(\mathchoice{Z^{{{+}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{+}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{+}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{+}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}+\mathchoice{Z^{{{-}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.63333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.16666pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{-}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.63333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.16666pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{-}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.63333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.16666pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{-}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.69446pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.63333pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.16666pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}})\,,

(5.72)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\parallel}{k}{l}}}}{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\parallel}{k}{l}}}}{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\parallel}{k}{l}}}}{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[ \displaystyle=\mathchoice{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}}{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}}{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}}{{\mathcal{Z}}^{{{\parallel}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\texts
