\displaystyle=h_{+}\tfrac{1}{2}(1+\cos^{2}\vartheta)\,,

\displaystyle\mathchoice{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{y}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{y}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{y}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{y}}}}

\displaystyle=h_{\times}\cos\vartheta\,,

(8.3)

as they determine the phase shift, the birefringence effect, and the deformation of the electromagnetic polarisation, respectively. Their angular dependence is depicted in
Figure

1
, where we plot only absolute values in order to obtain positive radii.

As expected, the phase shift vanishes whenever the gravitational wave propagates in the same or the opposite direction as the electromagnetic wave and is maximal when the two waves propagate in orthogonal directions.
The polarisation-dependent effects are maximal in the collinear case and are minimal for orthogonal propagation. Note that the deformation of linear polarisation to elliptic polarisation vanishes for orthogonal propagation, but the birefringence effect persists in this case. Moreover, the phase and birefringence terms have a definite sign (for fixed values of
\mathchoice{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{+}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{+}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{+}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.13892pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{+}}}}
and
\mathchoice{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\times}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\times}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\times}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\times}}}}
) but the polarisation term
\mathchoice{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{y}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{y}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{y}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{y}}}}
can have arbitrary sign. For example, if
\mathchoice{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\times}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\times}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\times}}}}{h^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[6.25002pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.81111pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.72223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\times}}}}
is positive, then the “right lobe” in
Figure

1
corresponds to positive
\mathchoice{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{y}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{y}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{y}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{y}}}}
, while the “left lobe” corresponds to negative values.

The order-of-magnitude of the overall effect caused by continuous
gw
’s emitted by a rotating neutron star can be estimated by

\varepsilon\approx 10^{-24}\left(\frac{e}{10^{-6}}\right)\left(\frac{I}{10^{38}\,\mathrm{kg}\,\mathrm{m}^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{f}{\mathrm{kHz}}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{d}{\mathrm{kpc}}\right)^{-1}

(8.4)

where
I
is the moment of inertia about the rotation axis,
e
the eccentricity parameter,
{f=\omega_{g}/2\pi}
the emitted frequency, and
d
is the distance to the source
[
37
,
38
]
.
(As no continuous
gw
’s have been detected so far, no precise values for these quantities are known, but the normalisations indicate plausible values.)
Using
c_{1}\approx\bar{n}\approx 1
, the amplitude of the phase perturbation is found to be approximately

\delta\psi\approx 2\times 10^{-6}\,{\text{µ}\mathrm{rad}}\times\left(\frac{e}{10^{-6}}\right)\left(\frac{I}{10^{38}\,\mathrm{kg}\,\mathrm{m}^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{f}{\mathrm{kHz}}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{d}{\mathrm{kpc}}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{\ell}{\mathrm{km}}\right)\,,

where
\ell
is the length of the optical fibre.
Relative to this phase perturbation, the amplitude perturbation effects are suppressed by an additional factor of
\Delta^{2}\approx(0.5\%)^{2}=2.5\times 10^{-5}
, so that we expect only the phase perturbation to be of experimental relevance. We leave an experimental feasibility assessment for future work, but to the best of our knowledge, this is beyond experimental reach in the foreseeable future.

9
Comparison with Light Propagation in Vacuum

Finally, we comment on the relation to standard results for light propagation in vacuum.
First, consider the phase (
1.2
) for
m=0
. In vacuum, the dispersion relation reduces to
\beta=\omega
and the phase parameter is
c_{1}=1
, so that the overall phase takes the form

\psi_{\text{vac}}=\mathchoice{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}\mathchoice{x^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{\mu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}-\tfrac{1}{2}\varepsilon\mathchoice{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}{z}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}{z}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}{z}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}{z}}}}\omega z\cos(\omega_{g}t)\,,

(9.1)

where
k=\omega(\mathrm{d}t-\mathrm{d}z)
.
This is in agreement with the standard formula

\psi^{(1)}=\frac{\mathchoice{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\mu}}}}\mathchoice{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\nu}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\nu}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\nu}}}}{k^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{\nu}}}}}{2\omega^{2}}\int_{\omega z}^{0}\mathchoice{h^{{{\mu}{\nu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{{\mu}{\nu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{{\mu}{\nu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{h^{{{\mu}{\nu}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.00928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.42052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.72893pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}(\mathchoice{x^{{{\rho}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{\rho}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{\rho}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.1 (9.2)

where
s\mapsto\mathchoice{x^{{{\rho}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{\rho}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{\rho}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{x^{{{\rho}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}+s\mathchoice{k^{{{\rho}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{k^{{{\rho}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{k^{{{\rho}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{k^{{{\rho}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.14899pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53337pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.80954pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}/\omega^{2}
is the light ray emitted from the injection point (
z=0
) at
s=-\omega z
and reaching the observation point at
s=0
, cf. Ref.
[
6
, Eq. (3.28a)]
.
For the particular case considered here, this integral evaluates to

\psi^{(1)}=-\frac{\omega\mathchoice{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}{z}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}{z}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}{z}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}{z}}}}}{\omega_{g}(1-\mathchoice{{\hat{\kappa}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}}}}{{\hat{\kappa}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}}}}{{\hat{\kappa}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}}}}{{\hat{\kappa}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}}}})}[\sin(\kappa.x)-\sin(\kappa.x-\omega_{g}z(1-\mathchoice{{\hat{\kappa}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}}}}{{\hat{\kappa}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}}}}{{\hat{\kappa}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}}}}{{\hat{\kappa}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}}}}))]\,,

(9.3)

and expanding to leading order in
\omega_{g}z\ll 1
reproduces (
9.1
).
(As already observed in Ref.
[
12
, Sect. 5]
, the second term is absent in Ref.
[
11
, Eq. (2.20)]
because of different boundary conditions used. We note that without this term the result is ill-behaved in the limit
\mathchoice{{\hat{\kappa}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}}}}{{\hat{\kappa}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}}}}{{\hat{\kappa}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}}}}{{\hat{\kappa}}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.10768pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.49423pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.7816pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{z}}}}\to 1
.)

Secondly, we note that
\Delta=(n_{1}-n_{2})/n_{1}
in (
1.3
) vanishes in vacuum, so that no polarisation perturbations arise at the order considered. This is in agreement with the geometrical optics setting, where such effects arise only at next-to-leading order in
\omega_{g}/\omega
, which is negligible in the considered setup.

Thus, the formulae presented here indeed reproduce the standard results for vacuum in the limit
n_{1}\to 1
and
n_{2}\to 1
.

10
Conclusion

We have solved perturbatively the Maxwell equations for a step-index waveguide in the presence of a weak gravitational wave (
gw
) of low frequency.
The correction of the central mode was found to describe a perturbation of the phase as expressed by (
6.11
).
The parameter
c_{1}
arising here was found to be well approximated by the effective refractive index
\bar{n}
: the relative error is smaller than the relative index difference
\varDelta=(n_{1}-n_{2})/n_{1}
.

Additionally, the “large sideband” (where the azimuthal mode index
m=\pm 1
is flipped) was identified as a perturbation of the electromagnetic wave polarisation. This perturbation for linearly polarised fields is given explicitly in (
7.14
), and for circular polarisation in (
7.15
).
Compared to the phase shift, this polarisation-dependent effect (describing birefringence and coupling of polarisation states) is suppressed by a factor of
\varDelta^{2}
.
However, contrary to the phase shift, this effect is also present when the gravitational wave propagates along the symmetry axis of the waveguide, as it is sensitive to the components
\tfrac{1}{2}(\mathchoice{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{x}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{x}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{x}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{x}}}}-\mathchoice{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{y}{y}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{y}{y}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{y}{y}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{y}{y}}}})
and
\mathchoice{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{y}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{y}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{y}}}}{A^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53473pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.80048pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.00034pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.30675pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.57817pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.84154pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{x}{y}}}}
of the gravitational wave amplitude.
Similar results for birefringence induced by gravitational waves were obtained for plane waves in infinitely extended media
[
39
,
40
]
, and also in vacuum for
exact
plane gravitational wave backgrounds
[
41
]
.

Further sidebands, where the azimuthal mode index is shifted by
\pm 1
or
\pm 2
, were also computed (equations (
5.115
) and (
5.116
)) but found to be negligibly small compared to the two main effects just mentioned: they are suppressed by the frequency ratio
\Omega=\omega_{g}/\omega
.

Comparing with light propagation in vacuum, we find that the waveguide dispersion enters in intermediate steps of the calculation, e.g. in (
5.105
) (which is structurally similar to equation (2.20) in Ref.
[
11
]
) via the term
\beta^{\prime}=\partial\beta/\partial\omega
. However, having implemented physically plausible boundary conditions modelling incoming radiation, we find that the dispersion term
\beta^{\prime}
cancelled in the final results for the phase correction (
6.11
) and for the perturbation of the polarisation (
7.14
).

Considering the calculations presented here in the context of previous calculations which determined the influence of Earth’s gravitational field and its rotation on fibre-modes, we conclude that the phase shifts in all three problems are well described using semi-heuristic arguments based on the eikonal equation in a “fictitious optical metric” where the discontinuous function
n
in (
3.4
) is replaced by the constant value of the effective refractive index
\bar{n}
(computed from the unperturbed problem). In all cases, the difference between such geometrical-optics estimates and the result obtained by solving the true field equations was essentially determined by the relative index difference
\varDelta\ll 1
.
In contrast, the perturbation of the polarisation found here goes beyond such simplistic models, and thus required a thorough analysis of Maxwell’s equations.

Apart from neglecting terms of order
\omega_{g}/\omega
(low-frequency regime) and
\rho\omega_{g}
(which is consistent since
\rho\omega\sim 1
), we have neglected the elastic response of the fibre to the gravitational wave, which influences light propagation therein due to the induced displacement of the core-cladding interface (where continuity conditions had to be imposed) and due to stress-induced changes of the optical properties of the materials.
Effects of the second kind were previously studied for transparent solid bars (elasto-optical antennas), see e.g. Refs.
[
42
,
43
,
44
,
45
]
, but such calculations have not been carried over to optical fibres yet.

Appendix A
Derivation of the Wave Equation

Here, we derive the wave equation for the electromagnetic field in the
gw
metric considered above, neglecting terms of order
\varepsilon^{2}
.
Throughout this section, we raise and lower indices with the
full
spatial metric
\mathchoice{\mathrm{g}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{i}{j}}}}{\mathrm{g}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{i}{j}}}}{\mathrm{g}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{i}{j}}}}{\mathrm{g}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{i}{j}}}}
.
Equation (

) can then be written as

(A.1)

where
\nabla
denotes the spatial covariant derivative.
Lowering the index, keeping in mind that the spatial metric is time dependent, one obtains the equivalent form

(A.3)

Here, no time derivatives of the volume form occur as the trace of the metric perturbation is time-independent, and no derivatives of the Christoffel symbols occur as the exterior derivative is independent of the connection.
Using
\mathchoice{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{j}}}}{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{j}}}}{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{j}}}}{\nabla^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{{j}}}}\mathchoice{Z^{{{j}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{j}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{j}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{j}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}=0
and the Ricci identity, we have

(A.4)

where
\mathchoice{R^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{R^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{R^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{R^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}
is the
spatial
Ricci tensor.
Inserting this into the previous equation then yields

n^{2}\mathchoice{\ddot{Z}^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\ddot{Z}^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\ddot{Z}^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\ddot{Z}^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}-\Delta\mathchoice{Z^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{i}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}+\mathchoice{R^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{R^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox (A.5)

Appendix B
Continuity Conditions

The jumps of the various field components are linear combinations of the various parameters
\alpha
and
\sigma
.
For example, if
w
denotes any field component

\left\llbracket{w}\right\rrbracket=a^{1}\alpha_{1}+a^{2}\alpha_{2}+s^{1}\sigma_{1}+s^{2}\sigma_{2}\,,

(B.1)

with some coefficients
a^{1},a^{2},s^{1},s^{2}
, for which we shall simply write

\left\llbracket{w}\right\rrbracket=(a^{1},a^{2})_{\alpha}+(s^{1},s^{2})_{\sigma}\,.

(B.2)

To analyse the jumps of the real fields
E,D,B,H
, we separate the
j
-real and
j
-imaginary parts.
Accordingly, we write

(a+jb,c+jd)_{\alpha}=[a+jb,c+jd,-b+ja,-d+jc]_{\alpha}

(B.3)

to state explicitly which coefficients multiply the
j
-real parameters
\Re\alpha_{1},\Re\alpha_{2},\Im\alpha_{1},\Im\alpha_{2}
.
The real and imaginary parts are then given by

\displaystyle\Re(a+jb,c+jd)_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=[a,c,-b,-d]_{\alpha}\,,

(B.4)

\displaystyle\Im(a+jb,c+jd)_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=[b,d,a,c]_{\alpha}\,.

(B.5)

Collecting the above mentioned real parameters of
\alpha
into one column vector

\alpha=\begin{pmatrix}\Re\alpha_{1}&\Re\alpha_{2}&\Im\alpha_{1}&\Im\alpha_{2}\end{pmatrix}^{\textsf{T}}\,,

(B.6)

and similarly for
\sigma
, we may express the dependence of
\left\llbracket{Z}\right\rrbracket
, as defined in (
5.69
), in matrix form

\left\llbracket{Z}\right\rrbracket=M\boldsymbol{\alpha}+\Sigma\boldsymbol{\sigma}\,,

(B.7)

where
M
and
\Sigma
are
j
-real matrices of dimension
4\times 4
.
In the perturbed case, we may similarly collect the real parameters of the
\sigma
’s into additional column vectors and add their contributions with additional matrices.

These “continuity matrices” fully describe the jumps of the fields at the core-cladding interface and are hence useful to implement continuity conditions. We discuss their general form and compute the matrix for the unperturbed problem explicitly.

B.1
General Structure of the Continuity Matrices

Since the electric and magnetic fields are derived from the single complex field
Z
defined in (
3.8
), the continuity matrices have only eight independent components.
This can be seen from the equations (
B.4
) and (
B.5
): if
w
denotes any field component, then
\left\llbracket{\Re w}\right\rrbracket
completely determines
\left\llbracket{\Im w}\right\rrbracket
.
More precisely, if the jumps of the real fields
\Re Z^{r}
and
\Re Z_{\parallel}
are given

\displaystyle\left\llbracket{\Re Z^{r}}\right\rrbracket

\displaystyle=[a,b,c,d]_{\alpha}\,,

\displaystyle\left\llbracket{\Re Z_{\parallel}}\right\rrbracket

\displaystyle=[e,f,g,h]_{\alpha}\,,

(B.8)

then the vector
\left\llbracket{Z}\right\rrbracket
, as defined in (
5.69
), is fully determined:

\begin{pmatrix}\left\llbracket{D^{r}}\right\rrbracket\\
\left\llbracket{E_{\parallel}}\right\rrbracket\\
\left\llbracket{B^{r}}\right\rrbracket\\
\left\llbracket{H_{\parallel}}\right\rrbracket\\
\end{pmatrix}=\begin{pmatrix}a&b&c&d\\
e/n_{1}^{2}&f/n_{2}^{2}&g/n_{1}^{2}&h/n_{2}^{2}\\
-c/n_{1}&-d/n_{2}&a/n_{1}&b/n_{2}\\
-g/n_{1}&-h/n_{2}&e/n_{1}&f/n_{2}\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}\Re\alpha_{1}\\
\Re\alpha_{2}\\
\Im\alpha_{1}\\
\Im\alpha_{2}\end{pmatrix}\,.

(B.9)

Thus, the first two rows (which describe
\left\llbracket{D^{r}}\right\rrbracket=\Re_{j}\left\llbracket{Z^{r}}\right\rrbracket
and
\left\llbracket{E_{\parallel}}\right\rrbracket=\Im_{j}\left\llbracket{n^{-2}Z_{\parallel}}\right\rrbracket
) determine the entire matrix
M
in
\left\llbracket{Z}\right\rrbracket=M\boldsymbol{\alpha}
. The form given here is in agreement with the abbreviating notation (
5.78
) up to redefinition of the parameters.

B.2
Continuity Matrix of the Unperturbed Problem

As an example, we calculate the continuity matrix for the unperturbed problem explicitly.
From (
4.9
) we have

i\zeta\mathchoice{Z^{{{{(0)}}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}=\rho^{2}\beta\partial_{r}\mathchoice{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{ mnr^{-1}\mathchoice{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\,.

(B.10)

Using (
4.8
), the jumps of
\mathchoice{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
and its radial derivative are readily found to be

\displaystyle\left\llbracket{\mathchoice{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}}\right\rrbracket

\displaystyle=(J_{m}(U),-K_{m}(W))\,,

(B.11)

\displaystyle\left\llbracket{\rho\partial_{r}\mathchoice{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{\parallel}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}}\right\rrbracket

\displaystyle=(UJ_{m}^{\prime}(U),-WK_{m}^{\prime}(W))\,.

(B.12)

Here, we omit the subscript
\alpha
, as the parameters
\sigma
do not enter the unperturbed problem.
From this, we find

\left\llbracket{i\zeta\mathchoice{Z^{{{{(0)}}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}}\right\rrbracket=\rho\beta(UJ_{m}^{\prime}(U),-WK_{m}^{\prime}(W))-ij\rho\omega m(n_{1}J_{m}(U),-n_{2}K_{m}(W))\,.

(B.13)

Since
\zeta
equals
+U^{2}
in the core and
-W^{2}
in the cladding, we deduce

\left\llbracket{\mathchoice{Z^{{{{(0)}}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{Z^{{{{(0)}}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}}\right\rrbracket=-i\rho\beta\left(\frac{1}{U}J_{m}^{\prime}(U),\frac{1}{W}K_{m}^{\prime}(W)\right)-j\rho\omega m\left(\frac{n_{1}}{U^{2}}J_{m}(U),\frac{n_{2}}{W^{2}}K_{m}(W)\right)\,,

(B.14)

and taking the
j
-real part using (
B.4
), we obtain

\left\llbracket{\mathchoice{D^{{{{(0)}}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{D^{{{{(0)}}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{D^{{{{(0)}}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{D^{{{{(0)}}{r}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}}\right\rrbracket=\left[-i\frac{\rho\beta}{U}J_{m}^{\prime}(U),-i\frac{\rho\beta}{W}K_{m}^{\prime}(W),mn_{1}\frac{\rho\omega}{U^{2}}J_{m}(U),mn_{2}\frac{\rho\omega}{W^{2}}K_{m}(W)\right]\,.

(B.15)

The jump of
E_{\parallel}=n^{-2}\Re Z^{\parallel}
is obtained directly from (
B.11
):

\left\llbracket{\mathchoice{E^{{{{(0)}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\parallel}}}}{E^{{{{(0)}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\parallel}}}}{E^{{{{(0)}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\parallel}}}}{E^{{{{(0)}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.09723pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.45pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.75pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[10.23616pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.2611pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.47223pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\parallel}}}}}\right\rrbracket=[n_{1}^{-2}J_{m}(U),n_{2}^{-2}K_{m}(W),0,0]\,.

(B.16)

The last two equations determine the unperturbed discontinuity matrix and the result is in agreement with
\Pi_{0}
given by equation (
5.80
).
